Result: 周期性 RoPE 的识别能力恰等同于带模块化谓词的过去时态逻辑（PTL+MOD）；传统 RoPE 则退化为基于精度的有界固定偏移查找的有限模拟，而非全长度模块化刻画。实验表明周期性调度可在模块化语言上长度泛化，而传统 RoPE 表现为有界局部性偏差，可能损害需要位置无关远端上下文访问的任务。

Conclusion: 周期性 RoPE 与传统 RoPE 在表达能力上存在本质分离，本研究弥合了理论表达性刻画与实际模型之间的鸿沟，为理解 RoPE 在不同任务中的行为差异提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Two accounts recur in explanations of the success of rotary position embeddings (RoPE). Expressivity studies associate periodic position information with modular predicates, whereas mechanistic and long-context studies emphasize positional anchors and local offsets. We formalize both accounts for fully uniform, finite-precision soft-attention transformers. We find that, if every rotary component is periodic, RoPE transformers recognize exactly the languages definable in past temporal logic with modular predicates. Conventional RoPE is different: The rotations it computes never repeat. This yields a precision-dependent bounded simulation of fixed-offset look-back operators, rather than an all-length modular characterization. Controlled experiments match this separation: Constructed periodic schedules length-generalize on modular languages, while conventional RoPE behaves more like a bounded locality bias and can impair tasks requiring position-invariant access to distant context. Altogether, our findings shed light on RoPE transformers, bringing theoretical expressivity characterizations closer to models used in practice.

</details>


### [245] [A Factor Graph Approach to Scalable Multi-Output Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11917)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Esther G. van Pelt,Albert Podusenko,İsmail Şenöz,Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多输出高斯过程回归表示为Forney风格因子图，通过最近邻链将候选输入集排序为一维序列，利用线性高斯转移因子和线性核心化（LMC）混合因子实现精确高斯消息传递，将计算复杂度从传统方法的立方级降低到O(C(DL²+L³))，并在低输入维度下紧追精确核矩阵后验，同时线性扩展至数据规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出高斯过程回归的计算复杂度随观测次数乘以输出数呈立方级增长，且当不同输出在不同输入点被观测时，密集核矩阵方法需要特殊处理。现有方法在大规模数据和缺失观测场景下计算代价高昂，亟需一种既能高效扩展又能保持后验精度的替代方案。

Method: 将多输出高斯过程回归表达为Forney风格因子图：首先利用最近邻链将固定候选输入集（C个）排序为一维序列；沿链使用线性高斯转移因子刻画潜在Matérn过程演化；通过确定性混合因子（线性模型核心化，LMC）将L个潜在过程混合为D个输出；每个输出配备标量观测因子。后验计算通过链上的精确高斯消息传递完成，复杂度为O(C(DL²+L³))，缺失观测只需省略对应局部因子，无需重构协方差矩阵。

Result: 在合成输入维度扫描实验中，因子图后验在低输入维度下紧密追踪精确核矩阵后验，随着输入维度升高差距逐渐增大，但仍与稀疏变分诱导点和最近邻基线保持竞争力。在电力时间序列预测任务中，因子图方法在预测精度上与三种基线持平，同时计算时间随数据点数线性扩展，而精确核矩阵方法已不可行，诱导点基线明显更慢。

Conclusion: 该因子图公式在数据样本数和缺失观测率方面具有良好的可扩展性，最适合低输入维度的候选集场景。在计算效率与后验精度之间取得了有效平衡，为大规模多输出高斯过程回归提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-output Gaussian process regression scales cubically in the number of observations times outputs, and dense kernel-matrix methods need bespoke handling whenever different outputs are observed at different inputs. We express multi-output Gaussian process regression as a Forney-style factor graph in which a nearest-neighbor chain orders a fixed candidate set of $C$ inputs into a one-dimensional sequence. Along this chain, latent Matérn processes evolve through linear-Gaussian transition factors, while the linear model of coregionalization mixes $L$ latent processes into $D$ outputs through a deterministic mixing factor and per-output scalar observation factors. Posterior computation reduces to exact Gaussian message passing on the chain at cost $\mathcal{O}(C(DL^2 + L^3))$ after chain construction, and missing observations omit their local factor without any covariance-matrix restructuring. The formulation therefore scales in the number of data samples and in the rate of missing observations, while remaining best suited to candidate sets in low input dimension.We compare the factor-graph formulation against an exact kernel-matrix baseline, a sparse-variational inducing-point baseline, and a nearest-neighbor baseline on a synthetic input-dimension sweep and on electricity time series forecasting. At low input dimension the factor-graph posterior tracks the exact kernel-matrix posterior closely, and the gap grows gradually as input dimension increases while staying competitive with both approximate baselines. On the electricity time series our factor-graph formulation matches all three baselines in forecast accuracy while scaling linearly in the number of data points, where the exact kernel-matrix method becomes infeasible and the inducing-point baseline remains substantially slower.

</details>


### [246] [Distillation of Foundation Models for Time-dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11937)
*Daniel Musekamp,Boshra Ariguib,Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TREX知识蒸馏框架，将预训练基础PDE模型的预测能力迁移到紧凑高效的学生模型中。通过教师模型生成合成轨迹扩充有限数据，学生模型可以匹配甚至超越教师精度，同时参数量减少几个数量级、推理速度提升一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础PDE模型虽在少数据场景下表现优异，但通常规模庞大且计算密集，限制了其作为数值求解器快速替代品的实用性。

Method: TREX首先微调教师模型，然后通过教师滚动推理生成长合成轨迹（可选周期性噪声注入）来扩充有限的域数据，使学生模型暴露于长时程状态和自回归预测中遇到的局部恢复行为，学生还可引入任务特定的归纳偏置（如等变性）。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，TREX训练的学生模型能够匹配甚至超越教师精度，参数量减少数个数量级，推理速度提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: TREX展示了通过知识蒸馏将大规模基础PDE模型压缩为高效学生模型的可行性，无需显式知晓初始条件分布即可采样教师诱导的滚动分布，为低数据场景下快速PDE代理模型提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Foundation models for time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are trained on large and diverse collections of physical systems and can generalize effectively to new downstream tasks. After fine-tuning on only a few trajectories from a target domain, they can achieve strong accuracy in low-data regimes. However, these models are typically large and computationally intensive, limiting their usefulness as fast surrogates for numerical solvers. We propose Teacher Rollout Extension (TREX), a knowledge distillation framework that transfers the predictive capability of a pretrained foundation model into a compact and efficient student. Starting from a fine-tuned teacher, TREX augments limited downstream data by generating long synthetic trajectories through teacher rollouts, optionally with periodic noise injection. This procedure samples from the teacher-induced rollout distribution without requiring explicit knowledge of the initial-condition distribution, while exposing the student to long-horizon states and local recovery behavior around states encountered during autoregressive prediction. The student can further incorporate task-specific inductive biases, such as equivariance, that the teacher does not necessarily enforce. We evaluate TREX on multiple PDE benchmarks. The resulting students can match or surpass the teacher's accuracy while reducing the number of parameters by several orders of magnitude and achieving more than an order-of-magnitude speedup in inference.

</details>


### [247] [TailBooster: A Dual-Layer Generative Framework for Extreme Value Augmentation with Operational Validity Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11951)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh,Jacco Hoekstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailBooster，一个双层生成框架，结合统计层（IQR提取极端值）和深度学习层（自编码器清洗），用于航空运输极端事件的数据增强。实验表明该方法显著提升了合成数据的操作有效性和极端事件预测效用，相比常规合成数据将MAE降低47-57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运输中的极端事件（严重延误、异常飞行时间）在历史记录中极为稀少，导致机器学习模型训练信号不足。现有生成模型存在两个缺陷：一是难以表示分布尾部，二是无法保证生成操作上不可行的实例（如短飞行时间搭配远距离）。目前没有方法能同时解决这两个问题，尤其针对混合类型表格数据。

Method: TailBooster采用双层生成框架：第一层是统计层，通过四分位距（IQR）提取极端值，为专用的生成模型（Tabular Variational Autoencoder）提供尾部集中训练信号；第二层是深度学习层，应用基于自编码器的清洗机制，丢弃违反从历史数据中学到的操作边界（operational envelope）的合成记录。

Result: 在美国航班记录上从多样性、统计相似性、保真度、操作有效性和效用五个维度评估。数据驱动的清洗显著提高了操作有效性，针对性增强提升了极端事件预测的效用。在六个回归算法中，相比常规合成数据，极端飞行时间预测的MAE降低了47-49%，极端到达延误预测降低了29-57%。将真实记录与合成极端值结合时也有类似的增益。

Conclusion: TailBooster完全数据驱动且与模型无关，可扩展至极端事件预测关键且缺乏领域规则的其他领域。

Abstract: Extreme events in air transport, such as severe arrival delays and abnormal air times, cause cascading network disruptions with substantial operational, economic, and safety costs. Such events are rare in historical records, leaving insufficient training signal for machine learning models. Synthetic data augmentation offers a principled solution, but conventional generative models under-represent distributional tails and give no guarantee against operationally infeasible instances, such as a short air time paired with a long flight distance. No existing approach addresses both limitations for mixed-type tabular records. We propose TailBooster, a dual-layer generative framework combining generative modelling with two anomaly detection layers. A statistical layer extracts extremes via the interquartile range, supplying tail-concentrated training signal to dedicated generative models, here a Tabular Variational Autoencoder. A deep learning layer then applies autoencoder-based cleaning, discarding synthetic records that violate the operational envelope learned from historical data. The framework was evaluated on US flight records across five dimensions: diversity, statistical similarity, fidelity, operational validity, and utility, the latter two being the primary improvement targets. Data-driven cleaning markedly improved operational validity, while targeted augmentation enhanced utility for extreme-event prediction. Across six regression algorithms, training on the framework's records reduced Mean Absolute Error by 47-49% on extreme air time and 29-57% on extreme arrival delay prediction relative to conventional synthetic data, with comparable gains when real records were enriched with synthetic extremes. Being fully data-driven and model-agnostic, TailBooster extends to domains where extreme-event prediction is critical and domain-specific rules are unavailable.

</details>


### [248] [LoongReflect: Boosting Long-Horizon Reflection in Search Agents via Global Perspective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11967)
*Zhixin Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Weixuan Xu,Guohong Qiu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoongReflect，一个将反思建模为记忆控制策略的训练框架。该框架在可逆轨迹树上使用显式的反思和回溯动作，结合快速通道（从特权教师蒸馏全局反思行为）和慢速通道（基于结果的GRPO优化）两种互补信号，通过前瞻性外梯度协调机制进行学习，在多项推理基准上超越仅基于结果的强化学习和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在长程推理中需要反思能力来评估轨迹进展、识别缺失证据和不可靠中间状态，并决定继续、修改或放弃当前分支。然而，反思在局部执行但效用取决于最终轨迹结果，这种局部-全局不匹配使得基于结果的强化学习只能为反思决策提供局部、稀疏且延迟的监督信号，学习有效的反思非常困难。

Method: LoongReflect将反思形式化为记忆控制策略，智能体在可逆轨迹树上通过显式的反思和回溯动作运作。反思将已验证事实、缺失证据和分支特定风险整合到工作记忆中，回溯则从活动上下文中移除不可靠分支并保留简洁的纠正性教训。训练采用前瞻性外梯度式协调机制结合两条通道：快速通道从特权教师蒸馏全局信息引导的反思行为（监督仅限于反思和回溯token），慢速通道使用基于结果的GRPO优化完整轨迹，使局部控制决策与最终任务成功对齐。

Result: 实验覆盖多跳检索增强生成和数学推理基准，LoongReflect相对于仅基于结果的强化学习和自蒸馏基线均展现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过区分反思/回溯token的局部监督与完整轨迹的全局监督，并以外梯度式协调机制融合两种信号，可以有效缓解反思学习的局部-全局监督不匹配问题，显著提升智能体的长程推理与反思能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on long-horizon reasoning to solve complex tasks involving planning, tool use, and memory. A critical capability in such settings is reflection: assessing trajectory progress, identifying missing evidence and unreliable intermediate states, and deciding whether to continue, revise, or abandon the current branch. Learning effective reflection, however, is challenging because reflection is performed locally within the current branch, whereas its utility can only be determined by its contribution to the final trajectory outcome. This local-global mismatch makes outcome-based reinforcement learning provide only local, sparse and delayed supervision for reflective decisions. To solve these, we propose LoongReflect, a training framework that formulates reflection as a memory-control policy. The agent operates over a reversible trajectory tree using explicit reflect and backtrack actions. Reflection consolidates verified facts, missing evidence, and branch-specific risks into working memory, while backtracking removes an unreliable branch from the active context and preserves a concise corrective lesson. To learn this policy, LoongReflect combines two complementary signals through a look-ahead, extragradient-style coordination mechanism. A fast channel distills globally informed reflective behavior from a privileged teacher, with supervision restricted to reflection and backtracking tokens. A slow channel optimizes complete trajectories using outcome-based GRPO, aligning local control decisions with final task success. Experiments on multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over outcome-only reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [249] [TESLA: Taylor Expansion of Sinusoidal Learnable Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11970)
*Daehwa Ko,Jaehyeon Kim,Seunghyun Ham,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TESLA激活函数，通过可学习的正弦和余弦项组合实现对多项式度数的显式控制，从而解决神经网络难以处理奇偶校验（parity）问题。在输入长度n=32的奇偶校验任务上，TESLA仅用100K训练样本（约输入空间的0.002%）即可获得强泛化能力，且在30%标签噪声下仍保持高准确率。此外，在ImageNet-100等视觉任务上表现与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇偶校验问题因线性不可分性和需要全局交互而一直是标准神经网络的挑战性任务。现有神经网络难以捕获这种高阶、全局的布尔结构，需要一种能够在激活层面显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高频分量的方法。

Method: 提出TESLA激活函数，定义为可学习的正弦与余弦项组合，能够显式控制多项式度数并选择性放大高阶分量。理论上，通过约束TESLA的系数可以获得Lipschitz/Rademacher复杂度界限，并使训练动态偏向强调高频结构。

Result: 在输入长度n=32的奇偶校验任务上，TESLA仅使用100K训练样本（约0.002%的输入空间）实现强泛化，并在30%标签噪声下保持高精度。与SIREN、SNAKE和Fourier特征嵌入等基线相比表现更优；在ImageNet-100上取得与现有方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: TESLA展示了激活级别的度数控制可以有效解决奇偶校验等高频结构学习问题，同时这种能力可以迁移到更通用的视觉任务中，为设计面向高频模式的神经网络激活函数提供了新思路。

Abstract: The parity problem--deciding whether the number of ones in a binary vector is odd or even--remains challenging for standard neural networks due to linear inseparability and the need for global interactions. We propose TESLA, an activation defined as a learnable combination of sine and cosine terms, enabling explicit control over polynomial degree and selective amplification of high-order components. Theoretically, we show that constraining TESLA's coefficients yields Lipschitz/Rademacher complexity bounds and shapes the training dynamics to emphasize higher-frequency structure. Empirically, on parity with input length n = 32, TESLA attains strong generalization with 100K training samples (approximately 0.002% of the 2^32 input space) and remains robust under heavy corruption, retaining high accuracy with up to 30% label noise. We also compare against periodic and frequency-based baselines (SIREN, SNAKE, and Fourier feature embeddings) on parity and Forrelation. Beyond synthetic structure, TESLA delivers comparable performance on ImageNet-100, indicating that activation-level degree control transfers to more general vision workloads. Code: https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/TESLA

</details>


### [250] [Remote Sensing and Machine Learning-Based Analysis of Land Use and Vegetation Change in Dhaka District, Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12001)
*Muhammad Masud Tarek,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muntasir Hasan Kanchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用遥感和机器学习技术，分析了孟加拉国达卡地区2019至2024年间土地利用和植被动态变化，发现城市建成区增长59.5%，植被和水体分别下降8.46%和7.77%，并验证了随机森林分类器的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达卡地区快速城市化导致土地利用和环境条件发生重大变化，亟需系统性监测以支持城市规划决策和生态可持续发展。

Method: 采用Sentinel-2 MSI和Landsat 8高分辨率卫星影像，基于Google Earth Engine平台，计算NDVI、NDBI和NDWI等光谱指数，运用决策树、KNN和随机森林三种监督机器学习分类器进行土地覆盖分类，并通过混淆矩阵和卡帕统计量评估精度。

Result: 五年间城市建成区面积增加59.5%，植被减少8.46%，水体减少7.77%；土地利用以植被和水体向城市基础设施转换为主导趋势；随机森林分类器在三种模型中精度最高。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了达卡地区无管制城市扩张带来的环境压力，证明了遥感与机器学习工具在提供及时、可操作数据以支持可持续城市发展、土地使用监管和生态保护政策方面的潜力。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization in Dhaka District, Bangladesh has triggered substantial alterations in land use and environmental conditions, necessitating systematic monitoring for informed urban planning and ecological sustainability. This study employs remote sensing data and machine learning techniques to analyze spatiotemporal changes in land cover and vegetation dynamics between 2019 and 2024. High-resolution satellite imagery from Sentinel-2 MSI and Landsat 8 was utilized to classify land cover types and compute spectral indices including the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), Normalized Difference Built-up Index (NDBI), and Normalized Difference Water Index (NDWI). A supervised machine learning approach incorporating Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and Random Forest classifiers was applied using labeled geospatial training points within Google Earth Engine. Accuracy assessments were conducted using confusion matrices and kappa statistics. Results indicate a 59.5% increase in urban built-up areas and a significant decline in vegetation (-8.46%) and water bodies (-7.77%) over the five-year period. Land conversion from vegetated and aquatic areas to urban infrastructure was identified as a dominant trend. Among the models, Random Forest demonstrated the highest classification accuracy. These findings underscore the growing environmental pressures driven by unregulated urban expansion in Dhaka. The study highlights the potential of remote sensing and machine learning tools in providing timely, actionable data to support sustainable urban development, land-use regulation, and ecosystem conservation policies.

</details>


### [251] [Dual-Model Sentiment Analysis of Consumer Reviews in the Retail Coffee Sector Using Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12007)
*Muntasir Hasan Kanchan,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muhammad Masud Tarek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Starbucks客户评论构建了对比情感分析框架，比较了五种传统机器学习模型（LR、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和五种深度学习模型（LSTM、RNN、双向LSTM、GRU、CNN）。在700多条来自ConsumerAffairs的评论数据上，SVM以91.0%的准确率居机器学习模型之首，双向LSTM在深度学习中表现最佳且泛化能力良好。研究还发现类别不平衡对正面情感召回率产生了负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者评论对品牌认知和商业策略至关重要，尤其是在零售咖啡等服务驱动型行业。现有研究缺乏对传统机器学习与深度学习方法在实际消费者情感分析场景中的系统性比较，因此有必要构建一个对比评估框架来指导模型选择和预处理策略。

Method: 数据集来自ConsumerAffairs，包含700多条Starbucks评论。通过预处理和探索性数据分析识别时间和地理模式，将1-3星标为负面、4-5星标为正面。分别评估五种机器学习分类器（Logistic Regression、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和五种深度学习模型（LSTM、RNN、双向LSTM、GRU、CNN），使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数进行性能评估。

Result: SVM在机器学习模型中准确率最高（91.0%），双向LSTM在深度学习中表现最强且在新数据上泛化良好。类别不平衡导致多个模型的正面情感召回率下降，数据集整体偏向负面情感。

Conclusion: 研究提供了机器学习与深度学习方法在实际消费者情感分析中的对比评估，强调了合理的模型选择和预处理对零售咖啡行业客户体验分析的重要性，并指出了类别不平衡对模型性能的显著影响。

Abstract: Consumer reviews play an important role in shaping brand perception and business strategies, particularly in service-driven industries such as retail coffee. This study presents a comparative sentiment analysis framework for Starbucks customer reviews using classical machine learning and deep learning approaches. The dataset, collected from ConsumerAffairs, contains more than 700 reviews and was analyzed through preprocessing and exploratory data analysis to identify temporal and geographic patterns. Sentiment labels were generated by binarizing star ratings, with ratings of 4 and 5 classified as positive and ratings of 1 to 3 as negative. The resulting dataset was substantially imbalanced toward negative sentiment. Five machine learning classifiers, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Decision Tree, Random Forest, and Naive Bayes, were evaluated alongside five deep learning models: LSTM, RNN, Bidirectional LSTM, GRU, and CNN. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score. SVM achieved the highest accuracy among the machine learning models at 91.0 percent, while Bidirectional LSTM showed the strongest performance among the deep learning models and demonstrated good generalization on unseen data. The findings also show that class imbalance negatively affected positive sentiment recall across several models. Overall, this study provides a comparative evaluation of machine learning and deep learning approaches for real-world consumer sentiment analysis and highlights the importance of appropriate model selection and preprocessing for customer experience analytics in the retail coffee sector.

</details>


### [252] [Reducing Symmetry Increase in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12010)
*Ning Lin,Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Wenbing Huang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了等变神经网络（ENN）在处理对称输入时出现的"对称性增强"现象，证明了该现象存在由特征空间结构决定的下确界，并提出了一套可计算的特征设计指南来有效抑制有害的对称性增强，通过合成数据集和QM9真实数据集验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络虽然在科学领域有广泛应用，但处理对称输入时存在表达能力退化的问题：输出表示会相对于输入产生额外的对称性增强。此前研究仅在特定情形下观察到这一现象，缺乏对其成因的严格理解和通用的抑制策略。

Method: (i) 对任意给定的特征空间和输入对称群，证明了对称性增强存在由特征空间结构决定的下确界；(ii) 开发了可计算的算法来求解该下确界，并提出用于特征设计的实用准则以防止有害的对称性增强；(iii) 在标准正则性假设下，证明了大多数等变映射都能通过该准则有效降低对称性增强。

Result: 通过合成数据集和真实世界QM9数据集上的实验与可视化，验证了理论预测的正确性，证明提出的特征设计准则能够有效降低对称性增强。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解等变神经网络的对称性增强现象提供了严格的数学刻画，并给出了可操作的特征设计框架，有助于在实际应用中提升ENN的表达能力和性能。

Abstract: Equivariant Neural Networks (ENNs) have empowered numerous applications in scientific fields. Despite their remarkable capacity for representing geometric structures, ENNs suffer from degraded expressivity when processing symmetric inputs: the output representations are invariant to transformations that extend beyond the input's symmetries. The mathematical essence of this phenomenon is that a symmetric input, after being processed by an equivariant map, experiences an increase in symmetry. While prior research has documented symmetry increase in specific cases, a rigorous understanding of its underlying causes and general reduction strategies remains lacking. In this paper, we provide a detailed and in-depth characterization of symmetry increase together with a principled framework for its reduction: (i) For any given feature space and input symmetry group, we prove that the increased symmetry admits an infimum determined by the structure of the feature space; (ii) Building on this foundation, we develop a computable algorithm to derive this infimum, and propose practical guidelines for feature design to prevent harmful symmetry increases. (iii) Under standard regularity assumptions, we demonstrate that for most equivariant maps, our guidelines effectively reduce symmetry increase. To complement our theoretical findings, we provide visualizations and experiments on both synthetic datasets and the real-world QM9 dataset. The results validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [253] [SoftWater: Class-Aware Rate Allocation for Softmax Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12026)
*Joao V. Cavalcanti,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftWater方法，将softmax输出层的量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题，利用类别感知的几何结构和可分离近似实现高效量化。在1B到32B的五个模型上，SoftWater在60个测试点中的59个上优于基线WaterSIC，将头部引起的KL散度降低6.5-8.3倍，2比特头部在量化模型上仅增加2.9-3.7%的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化流程通常将softmax输出层保留在高精度，但在词汇表较大的小型LLM中，输出头占据全部参数的15-30%，名义上的"2比特"模型实际位宽远高于此。因此需要对softmax层进行有效量化以减少存储负担。

Method: 将softmax层量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题；通过二阶分析揭示类别感知的几何结构，量化误差由特征协方差和类别特定softmax曲率联合加权；采用可分离近似将Kn×Kn的Cholesky分解替换为n×n因子分解并逐类缩放，通过逐次干扰消除进行格点编码；利用单次前向传播获取统计量。

Result: 在五个模型（1B到32B）上，SoftWater在60个测试点中的59个上优于WaterSIC，头部诱导KL降低6.5-8.3倍（2比特）；在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上，2比特头部减少45-60%存储字节，仅增加2.9-3.7%困惑度；绑定模型上4比特头部近乎无损，2比特损失低于4%。

Conclusion: SoftWater使softmax输出层的量化变得实用，尤其在绑定（tied）模型中表现优异，结合校准数据匹配部署领域可达到最低KL，使此类模型的头部量化具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization pipelines routinely leave the softmax output layer in high precision. Yet in small LLMs with modern vocabularies, the head holds 15--30\% of all parameters, so a nominal ``2-bit'' model with an fp16 head can store several times as many bits per weight. We pose softmax-layer quantization as a rate-distortion problem under the KL divergence between the original and quantized output distributions. A second-order analysis reveals a class-aware geometry: quantization error is weighted jointly by feature covariance and class-specific softmax curvature. A separability approximation replaces the $Kn\times Kn$ Cholesky with one $n\times n$ factorization rescaled per class, making the lattice encodable by successive interference cancellation, with both statistics from a single forward pass. The resulting method, SoftWater, gives fine grids to frequent, low-variance classes and coarse grids to rare ones, a large gap under Zipfian token distributions. Across five models from 1B to 32B, SoftWater outperforms the released WaterSIC quantizer (near-optimal under linear-layer WMSE but not output KL) at matched head rates on 59 of 60 test points, using none of that pipeline's refinements and cutting head-induced KL by $6.5\times$--$8.3\times$ at 2 bits. On Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct with quantized bodies, a 2-bit head removes 45--60\% of stored bytes for a $2.9$--$3.7\%$ perplexity increase. Because the class-side statistic comes from calibration data, matching calibration to the deployment domain gives the lowest KL on that domain throughout. On a tied model, a 4-bit head is near-lossless and a 2-bit head costs under 4\% perplexity, making head quantization of such models practical.

</details>


### [254] [Uncertainty-Aware Probabilistic Constrained Clustering from Entangled Pairwise Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12027)
*Shaojie Zhang,Ke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了不确定性感知的概率约束聚类（UPCC）框架，针对现实场景中成对约束为实值且带有噪声/模糊性的问题，设计了ECI-PP框架（估计器-校正器-积分器），通过角向成对目标函数和信念估计、校正、可靠性加权集成，在多个基准上超越现有深度约束聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度约束聚类（DCC）方法主要处理硬标签（必须连接/不能连接），但现实中的成对监督往往是实值的，且包含内在模糊性、专家判断和随机噪声。已有方法仅从数值层面处理软标签，缺乏语义层面的建模。

Method: 首先形式化不确定性感知概率约束聚类（UPCC）问题，定义基于异构观测过程的规范偶然目标并分析条件可辨识性；然后提出ProbPair角形成对目标函数用于概率关系建模；最后构建ECI-PP框架，包含估计器（belief estimation）、校正器（correction）和积分器（reliability-aware integration）三大模块，用于细化和整合不完美的监督信息。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的概率监督设置和多样化基准上的实验表明，ECI-PP在性能上优于现有最先进的深度约束聚类（DCC）方法，并且在共享默认配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文首次在语义层面系统处理了深度约束聚类中的概率（软）成对约束，通过形式化的UPCC框架和ECI-PP方法有效应对了实际监督数据中的噪声与不确定性，为该领域提供了新的理论分析和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise constrained clustering typically relies on hard must-link/cannot-link labels, whereas realistic pairwise supervision may be real-valued and entangle intrinsic ambiguity, expert judgment, and stochastic corruption. Existing deep constrained clustering (DCC) methods mainly target hard, expert-agnostic constraints, treating soft labels mostly numerically rather than semantically. We formalize this setting as uncertainty-aware probabilistic constrained clustering (UPCC), defining a canonical aleatoric target through a heterogeneous observation process and analyzing its conditional identifiability. We introduce ProbPair, an angular pairwise objective for probabilistic relations, and build ECI-PP, an estimator--corrector--integrator framework that refines imperfect supervision via belief estimation, correction, and reliability-aware integration. Across challenging probabilistic supervision settings, experiments on diverse benchmarks show that ECI-PP outperforms state-of-the-art DCC methods and remains robust with a shared default configuration.

</details>


### [255] [Clustered Randomized Smoothing for Stochastic Prediction Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12037)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Frederik Mathiesen,Julian Schumann,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类α平滑（clustered α-smoothing）框架，通过将噪声样本聚类后在各簇内局部应用α平滑，再将预测组合为混合分布，解决了传统随机平滑在随机多模态回归中因模式坍缩导致的鲁棒性失效问题，并在轨迹预测和四旋翼控制两个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代随机预测器能够建模丰富的多模态结果分布，但在安全关键领域中确保预测的鲁棒性至关重要。随机平滑是提升鲁棒性的主流技术，然而在多模态回归场景中，随机平滑常因模式坍缩（mode collapse）而失效，生成的均值预测无法反映真实的底层分布。

Method: 提出聚类α平滑框架，包含三个步骤：（1）使用任意聚类算法对噪声样本进行划分；（2）在每个簇内局部应用α平滑；（3）将各簇的预测结果组合成一个混合分布。通过将平滑分布解释为多个α平滑器的混合，推导出平滑预测落在多个不同模式对应紧致区域并集内的概率下界。

Result: 在两个基准上进行了实证评估：在驾驶模拟器数据集的随机轨迹预测任务中，与α平滑相比，预测分布与真实分布之间的Wasserstein距离平均降低27%；在四旋翼控制任务中，与最先进的随机平滑方法相比，碰撞率降低了81%。

Conclusion: 聚类α平滑通过保留多模态结构并在每个模态内分别进行平滑化，有效克服了传统随机平滑在多模态回归中的模式坍缩问题，为安全关键场景下的鲁棒多模态预测提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [256] [Towards Truly Unsupervised Evaluation of Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12057)
*Hafiz Saud Arshad,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地讨论了现有所谓"无监督"的特征选择评估方法实际上并非真正无监督，并提出了一种利用主成分分析和最优传输的全新真正无监督评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数用于无监督评估特征选择算法的方法存在关键设计缺陷，这些方法本质上是有监督评估在无监督下游任务下的变体，并非真正无监督，因此需要一种真正无需标签信息的评估方法。

Method: 提出一种全新的真正无监督评估框架，利用无监督主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）来衡量特征选择方法的质量，在整个评估过程中完全不需要任何标签信息。

Result: 论文通过批判性分析揭示了既有评估方法的本质缺陷，并展示了新提出的基于PCA和最优传输的评估框架能够在真正无监督的条件下有效衡量特征选择算法的质量。

Conclusion: 本文指出了现有无监督特征选择评估方法的根本问题，并提供了一种真正无监督的替代评估框架，为特征选择领域的评估方法提供了新的方向和解决方案。

Abstract: Feature selection is one of the most important and fundamental tasks in data mining, tackled by a family of methods with an established set of evaluation techniques to measure the quality of a specific method. Most of the methods commonly used for the unsupervised evaluation of feature selection algorithms suffer from critical design flaws which question their unsupervised nature. In this paper, we provide a critical discussion on the established allegedly unsupervised evaluation techniques, and shed light on the reasons why they are not truly unsupervised but, at best, supervised evaluation under an unsupervised downstream task. We also propose a novel, truly unsupervised evaluation framework to measure the quality of the feature selection algorithms without any form of information about the labels. The proposed framework utilizes unsupervised Principal Component Analysis, and optimal transport to measure the quality of the feature selection methods in a truly unsupervised manner.

</details>


### [257] [Faithful, Sufficient and Understandable: Rethinking Graph Counterfactual Explanations via Discrete Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12083)
*David Bechtoldt,Sidney Bender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GDCE-I，一种基于离散去噪扩散模型的反事实解释方法，通过新颖的离散反演方案在完整编辑空间中生成分布一致的图反事实编辑，并在统一评估框架下大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在多个领域表现出色但缺乏内在可解释性，限制了其在高风险场景中的应用。图反事实解释需要寻找最小结构修改来改变预测，但搜索空间是离散组合的，且需满足节点/边类型和领域规则（如化学价）。现有方法要么偏离数据流形，要么搜索不覆盖完整编辑空间。

Method: 提出GDCE-I方法：利用离散去噪扩散模型作为生成先验，通过新颖的离散反演方案（discrete inversion scheme）将目标图反演到潜在空间，使得生成的编辑既保持数据流形分布，又能覆盖整个领域的编辑空间。同时推导了一个包含反事实解释所需desiderata的评估框架，并设计了统一协议来公平比较各方法。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，GDCE-I在定义的评估框架下大幅优于现有方法。在分子领域，定性分析显示GDCE-I能够生成可解释且分布内（in-distribution）的反事实解决方案。

Conclusion: GDCE-I通过离散扩散模型和反演技术，同时解决了现有图反事实解释方法中"保持数据流形"与"覆盖完整编辑空间"不可兼得的问题，并提供了更全面、一致的评估框架。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on graph-structured data across domains such as chemistry, biology, and network analysis, yet they provide no intrinsic explanation of their predictions. This limits their adoption in high-stakes and safety-critical settings. Counterfactual explanations address this by revealing the minimal structural modifications that would change a model's prediction. On graphs, however, such a modification is hard to produce. The search space is discrete and combinatorial, and a valid answer must respect categorical node and edge types together with domain rules such as chemical valency in the case of molecular graphs. Existing explainers give up one of two things. Either edits are not held on the data manifold, or the search does not span the full edit space. We propose Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I), which gives up neither. A discrete denoising diffusion model with a novel discrete inversion scheme enables distribution-aware edits leveraging the whole domain edit space. We further address the incomplete and inconsistent evaluation of graph counterfactuals by deriving a framework of explanation desiderata and applying it to every method under one shared protocol. Across four benchmarks, GDCE-I outperforms related work by a large margin on the defined framework. For the molecular domain, we further qualitatively show that GDCE-I attains interpretable in-distribution solutions.

</details>


### [258] [NAE: Normalizing AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12084)
*Muhammad Abdur Rafae,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有近似逆的归一化流（flow autoencoders），通过理论分析证明现有方法的损失函数存在次优性，提出了被称为归一化自编码器（NAE）的新型条件损失，在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流自编码器（flow autoencoders）的损失函数设计是次优的，编码器和解码器代理（surrogate）必须与重建损失对齐进行优化。作者希望通过理论分析指导损失函数设计，以提升生成模型的性能。

Method: 作者对流自编码器的训练动态进行理论分析，证明现有损失函数的次优性，并提出归一化自编码器（NAE），采用一种新颖的条件损失，使代理损失梯度与重建损失梯度对齐，直接改进现有标准方法。该方法涵盖全维度（d=D）和瓶颈（d<D）两种设置。

Result: 在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准等多个任务上的大量实验表明，NAE达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 本文强调了损失对齐在流自编码器中的重要性，并将NAE确立为一个强大的生成框架，为该领域未来的损失设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider the setting of Normalizing flows with approximate inverses, an established paradigm spanning both full-dimensional ($d=D$) and bottleneck ($d<D$) settings, and group these models under the term flow autoencoders. We present a theoretical investigation into their training dynamics and prove that the proposed loss used by existing approaches is suboptimal; specifically, both encoder and decoder surrogates must be optimized in alignment with reconstruction loss. Guided by these insights, we propose Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE), which employs a novel conditional loss that aligns the surrogate loss gradient with that of reconstruction loss, directly improving upon the current standard. Extensive experiments across molecule generation, tabular data, and image benchmarks demonstrate that NAE achieves state of the art performance. Our work highlights the importance of loss alignment in flow autoencoders and establishes NAE as a powerful generative framework.

</details>


### [259] [Task- and dataset-specific information in protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12090)
*Roman Joeres,Ilya Senatorov,Olga V. Kalinina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了13个蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在15个下游任务中的中间层嵌入表示，发现PLMs的最后一层嵌入很少能取得最佳下游任务性能，并揭示了任务类型与信息分布在层间的关系：残基级任务的信息随层数递增，而整蛋白任务的性能更多由数据集特性决定，而非任务本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型已被广泛用于将氨基酸序列转换为潜在空间嵌入，但研究者普遍默认使用最后一层嵌入，而模型的内部行为仍缺乏深入理解。因此需要系统探究中间层嵌入的信息含量及其对下游任务性能的影响。

Method: 对13个PLMs和15个下游任务（来自11个数据集）进行系统分析。对每一层的嵌入训练探针模型并比较其性能，同时计算潜空间特征以估计嵌入所包含的信息量，从而揭示层间信息分布规律。

Result: 发现PLMs最后一层嵌入很少产生最佳下游任务性能。残基级任务的预测目标与预训练目标相似时，性能随层数稳定提升；整蛋白任务的性能主要由数据集决定，含深度突变扫描（DMS）数据的浅层嵌入表现更好，而含多样天然蛋白的数据集在深层嵌入上表现最佳。此外，PLMs在人工蛋白任务上的性能显著下降。

Conclusion: PLMs的层间信息分布并非简单的"越深越好"，选择嵌入层应根据下游任务类型和数据集特性而定。该发现对蛋白质语言模型的实际应用具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have transferred the latest advances from natural language processing to computational biology. These models, trained on large corpora of protein sequence data, are widely used to translate amino acid sequences into latent-space embeddings, ready for use in diverse downstream tasks (DTs). By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [260] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对高可靠性应用中的置信度校准问题，在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种现实场景下开发了校准方法：提出了基于噪声模型的噪声感知校准框架、针对温升预测的噪声兼容方法、无标签目标域的精度估计方法，以及本地差分隐私的共形预测框架，实现了在非理想数据条件下的可靠不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设存在干净的验证数据，但在实际场景中，标签噪声和域偏移普遍存在，导致校准效果失真。同时，隐私保护需求也未被充分考虑。因此，需要开发在非理想条件下仍然有效的校准方法，以确保深度模型在安全关键场景中的可靠部署。

Method: （1）针对标签噪声：利用估计的噪声模型重建无噪声置信度估计，并通过建模噪声与干净标签分布之间的关系扩展到共形预测，估计干净的符合性分数。（2）针对无监督域适应：从源域性能和域差异估计目标域精度，实现无需目标标签的校准。（3）针对隐私保护：提出本地差分隐私共形预测框架，在保护用户标签和模型输出的同时维持有效的不确定性量化。

Result: 论文提出的方法在三个维度上取得了成果：噪声感知校准方法在标签噪声下保持可靠的置信度估计；无监督域适应校准方法无须目标标签即可实现有效校准；局部差分隐私共形预测框架在隐私保障与预测可靠性之间取得了平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究弥合了校准理论与安全关键实际部署之间的差距，为噪声鲁棒、隐私保护和可靠性的神经网络预测提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个联邦学习框架，通过在函数空间中基于NTK的一致性评分来选择每个客户的个性化聚合更新集合，并采用P2P拓扑在私有验证数据上评估模型，在五个数据集和九个基线方法上持续优于集中式FL基线和现有P2P方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中确定哪些客户端更新对聚合有益是一个核心挑战。现有方法通常在参数空间中比较模型参数或梯度，但参数空间相似性在异质非IID数据下不能很好地反映预测行为，导致与目标域不一致的更新可能降低本地模型性能。

Method: LIGHTYEAR框架在函数空间中执行更新选择，使用NTK（神经正切核）一致性评分来刻画预测行为，为每个客户端确定个性化聚合集合。由于集中式FL在聚合前无法获取函数空间信息，LIGHTYEAR采用P2P拓扑，让客户端直接交换更新并在私有验证数据上评估传入模型，仅选择对自身目标域有益的更新，并使用正则化聚合规则提高异质性下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集和九个基线方法上，LIGHTYEAR一致性地优于集中式FL基线和现有的P2P方法。

Conclusion: 相比参数空间相似性，基于NTK的函数空间一致性评分提供了更具表达力的更新选择标准，能够有效应对异质非IID数据下客户端更新错位的问题，提升本地模型性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [262] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用SPAR方法将脉搏波时间序列（来自PPG和动脉张力测量）转换为图像，并用卷积神经网络将健康成年人分为两个相近年龄组（35-40岁和50-55岁），在内部和外部测试集上均取得70%以上的F1分数，证明SPAR衍生的脉搏波图像包含具有判别力的形态学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形态随年龄演变，反映心血管系统结构和功能变化，血管年龄是心血管健康的重要替代指标。早期血管老化提示疾病风险增加，脉搏波分析有望支持无症状成年人的风险分层，因此需要开发有效的分析方法来捕获脉搏波中的年龄相关信息。

Method: 使用SPAR方法将来自光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力测量的脉搏波时间序列数据转换为图像，然后使用这些SPAR图像训练卷积神经网络（CNN），对健康受试者进行分类，分为两个相近的年龄组（35-40岁和50-55岁）。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均表现出稳定的分类性能，对PPG和张力测量信号均取得了70%以上的F1分数。

Conclusion: SPAR衍生的脉搏波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成年人中也包含可区分的形态学特征。这项概念验证为未来利用SPAR结合智能可穿戴设备进行早期风险检测的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [263] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一般拟阵约束下的非负子模最大化问题，提出了对抗鲁棒性定理，证明SGS-Poisson算法在受控值预言机扰动下仍保持1/e（非单调）和1-1/e（单调）的极限近似因子，并由此得到全bandit CMAB算法的精确极限近似-遗憾因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模最大化算法通常假设值预言机精确无误，但实际中预言机可能受到任意控制误差的扰动。需要研究算法在受控或误差下的鲁棒性，以及这种鲁棒性如何转化为在线学习场景中的遗憾保证。

Method: 提出Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process（SGS-Poisson）算法，通过对抗韧性定理证明：在不修改泊松强度、单元素交换规则或恶意丢弃步骤的前提下，算法对任意受控预言机的扰动（误差上界为ξ）仍保持所述近似因子，复杂度为Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次预言机调用，并通过离线到在线的归约获得CMAB全bandit算法。

Result: 算法在任意受控预言机下，期望值至少达到(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)（非单调）和(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)（单调），最终获得Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})的遗憾上界。

Conclusion: SGS-Poisson算法对预言机扰动具有对抗鲁棒性，且该鲁棒性可直接转化为一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全bandit CMAB算法，取得精确的极限近似-遗憾权衡。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [264] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYDRA是一种参数高效的KAN超几何扩展方法，通过将输入映射到Poincaré球的有界双曲潜在空间，在切空间执行KAN式更新，并采用低秩原型块共享功能变换以减少参数冗余，在八个基准数据集上取得了竞争性或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN虽然通过用可学习的单变量函数替代标量权重增强了非线性函数逼近能力，但每个连接都分配独立函数导致大量参数冗余，限制了其可扩展性和效率。

Method: HYDRA将向量值输入映射到有界双曲潜在空间（Poincaré球），在切空间中执行KAN风格的更新，并使用低秩原型块在隐藏维度间共享功能变换。通过半径控制防止边界饱和，提高训练稳定性。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，HYDRA在保持竞争性或更优预测性能的同时，提高了参数效率，并增强了表示的的可解释性（通过提供结构化的径向坐标）。

Conclusion: HYDRA成功地将双曲几何融入KAN架构，在参数效率、预测性能和表示可解释性之间取得了良好的平衡，验证了双曲表示与样条功能学习结合的有效性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [265] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScreenShot是一个层次化Transformer模型，在大规模药物筛选数据集上预训练，通过上下文学习从少量功能测量中预测新患者对联合用药的反应，无需微调和分子谱分析，并在4个测试集上优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合用药可降低耐药风险，但组合筛选空间巨大，传统方法成本高、耗时长且技术上常不可行；现有预测模型需要分子谱分析和每队列训练，在时间和组织样本稀缺时应用受限。

Method: 构建层次化Transformer架构，在40个药物筛选数据集（涵盖3,700种药物和6,000个生物样本）上预训练；通过上下文学习直接从功能测量预测新患者对联合治疗的响应，无需微调和分子谱分析；利用模型内部表征驱动加权k-means++主动学习策略优化实验选择。

Result: 在4个保留测试集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗识别方面均优于所有基线；通过主动学习策略，仅用均匀筛选三分之一的预算即可实现相同的命中检测效果。

Conclusion: ScreenShot展示了无需分子谱分析和微调即可跨数据集泛化的能力，其内部表征还可用于实验设计优化，大幅降低联合用药筛选成本，为个性化联合治疗预测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>


### [266] [An Efficient Near-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial $m$-Set Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12231)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性组合老虎机问题，其中学习者每轮从d个物品中选m个，仅观察所选物品的聚合损失。提出了一个计算高效且无需显式枚举动作集的算法，保证以至少1-δ的概率获得O(√(dT log(K/δ)))的遗憾界，与有限动作集EXP3-KW算法的高概率界匹配，解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性组合老虎机中动作集大小为K=binom(d,m)，可能呈指数增长，导致直接枚举动作集在计算上不可行。虽然已有算法（如EXP3-KW）在遗憾界方面表现优异，但其直接实现可能需要指数级空间。因此需要设计既能保持相同遗憾上界、又能在多项式时间内运行的算法。

Method: 提出一个计算高效的算法，利用动作损失由同一d维物品损失向量决定的底层结构，避免显式枚举动作集。每个采样分布仅用d个参数表示，在多项式时间内运行。

Result: 针对自适应非预期对手，算法以至少1-δ的概率保证遗憾界为R_T = O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，与有限动作集EXP3-KW算法的高概率遗憾界匹配，且算法在多项式时间内运行。

Conclusion: 该算法在不牺牲遗憾性能的前提下解决了计算效率问题，以d个参数表示采样分布，无需枚举指数级动作集，从而解答了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study adversarial combinatorial bandits with $m$-set actions, where at each round the learner selects $m$ out of $d$ items and observes only the aggregate loss of the selected items. The resulting action set contains $K=\binom{d}{m}$ elements and can therefore be exponentially large. Nevertheless, the loss of every action is determined by the same $d$-dimensional vector of item losses. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits this structure without explicitly enumerating the action set. Against adaptive non-anticipating adversaries, it guarantees, with probability at least $1-δ$, regret against the best fixed action of \[
  R_T =
  O\left(\sqrt{dT\log(K/δ)}\right). \] This matches the high-probability regret bound of the finite-action EXP3-KW algorithm of Zimmert and Lattimore, whose direct implementation may require exponential space. Our algorithm instead represents each sampling distribution with $d$ parameters and runs in polynomial time without enumerating the action set. Thus, it resolves the open problem posed by Maiti et al.

</details>


### [267] [Calibration Bets on the Past: Post-Training Quantization for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12259)
*Junyi Ye,Ivy Gateri Wanjiku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了金融预测模型在后训练量化（PTQ）中激活校准的影响，发现8位量化下激活校准影响甚微，但在4位量化下成为预测性能的主要决定因素。基于S&P 500横截面波动率预测的560个训练模型评估表明，用百分位校准替代绝对最大值校准可恢复53-94%的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测模型在生产部署中常需低精度推理以降低内存和计算成本，但激活量化需要基于历史数据进行校准，且校准后在未来推理中保持固定。这一部署选择对金融预测的重要性此前未被充分理解。

Method: 在S&P 500横截面波动率预测任务上，对七种代表性神经网络架构、八个walk-forward测试年份（2018-2025年）和560个训练模型进行系统性评估，比较不同激活校准方法（绝对最大值与百分位校准）在8位和4位量化下的性能表现。

Result: 8位量化下激活校准影响很小，但4位量化下成为预测性能的决定性因素。默认abs-max校准下，4位静态权重量化加激活量化使受影响架构的全精度平均信息系数降低11-62%；改用百分位校准可恢复四个受影响最严重架构中53-94%的性能退化。最优激活范围随市场时期变化，窄范围在典型市场条件下表现更好，但测试期市场离散度超出校准历史时会失去部分优势。

Conclusion: 激活校正是金融预测中可靠4位PTQ部署的一等公民决策。当性能退化仍然显著时，8位激活或仅权重量化4位是更稳健的部署选择。

Abstract: Financial forecasting models are typically developed in full precision, yet production deployment often requires low-precision inference to reduce memory and computational cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables such deployment without retraining. However, reliable activation quantization requires calibration: activation ranges are estimated from historical data before deployment and then remain fixed during future inference. The importance of this deployment choice for financial forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of activation calibration for PTQ in cross-sectional volatility forecasting on the S&P 500. Our evaluation covers seven representative neural architectures, eight walk-forward test years (2018-2025), and 560 trained models. We find that activation calibration has little effect at 8 bits but becomes the primary determinant of predictive performance at 4 bits. Under default absolute-maximum (abs-max) calibration, static 4-bit quantization of both weights and activations removes 11-62% of the full-precision mean information coefficient in affected architectures. Replacing abs-max with percentile calibration recovers 53-94% of this degradation in the four most affected architectures. The preferred activation range also varies across market periods. Narrow ranges improve resolution under typical market conditions but lose part of their advantage when test-period market dispersion exceeds the calibration history. These findings show that activation calibration is a first-class deployment decision for reliable 4-bit PTQ in financial forecasting. When substantial degradation remains, 8-bit activations or weight-only 4-bit quantization provide more robust deployment choices.

</details>


### [268] [Earth observation embeddings are effective sub-grid descriptors for probabilistic weather downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12271)
*Pedro Sousa,Will Tebbutt,Sadiq Jaffer,Robin Young,Anil Madhavapeddy,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用地球观测基础模型（TESSERA）的嵌入特征作为局部地表描述符，增强卷积条件神经过程进行概率天气降尺度。该方法将~25km的ERA5粗网格场降尺度到站点尺度，在五个气候区、空间和时间双重留出验证下，2米温度和10米风速的CRPS技能分别提升11.5%和6.2%。这是首个证明长期地球观测嵌入能支持短期天气降尺度的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气再分析数据在粗网格上解析大气状态，但站点级应用需要任意位置的高分辨率预测，而近地表条件还取决于未解析的地形和地表属性。现有概率降尺度方法依赖手工设计的地形描述符，缺乏可迁移的通用地表表示。

Method: 将卷积条件神经过程（convolutional conditional neural process）与TESSERA基础模型提取的10m分辨率局部地表嵌入结合。具体做法是将TESSERA嵌入块压缩为学习的局部地表描述符，用于增强粗网格ERA5（~25km）的降尺度过程。

Result: 在五个气候多样的区域、空间和时间双重留出验证下，嵌入特征将2米温度CRPS技能提升11.5%，10米风速提升6.2%。地形对温度亚网格结构的解释力更强，而TESSERA为风速提供额外地表信息。改进在输入从ERA5换成Aurora AI预报模型以及预测无区域历史的新部署站点时仍然保持。

Conclusion: 研究首次证明长期时间尺度的地球观测嵌入能够支持短期天气降尺度，其中亚网格偏差系统性地由持久地表属性构成。这为概率天气降尺度提供了可迁移的通用地表表示新范式。

Abstract: Global weather reanalyses and forecasts resolve the evolving atmospheric state on coarse grids, but site-specific applications require predictions at arbitrary locations where near-surface conditions also depend on unresolved terrain and land-surface properties. Existing probabilistic downscalers address this gap using hand-crafted topographic descriptors. We ask instead whether Earth observation foundation models can provide transferable sub-grid surface representations for probabilistic weather downscaling.
  We augment a convolutional conditional neural process that downscales coarse ERA5 reanalysis fields at ~25 km resolution with a learned local surface descriptor, obtained by compressing a patch of TESSERA embeddings at 10 m resolution. Although these embeddings summarise surface conditions over annual timescales, they improve downscaling of instantaneous 2 m temperature and 10 m wind speed by encoding persistent surface properties that capture a location's departure from the coarse-grid atmospheric state. Across five climatically diverse regions, the embedding improves point and probabilistic skill at stations held out in both space and time, overall improving CRPS skill by 11.5% for 2 m temperature and 6.2% for 10 m wind speed. We further analyse how its contribution differs by variable, finding that topography explains more of temperature's sub-grid structure, while TESSERA provides additional surface information for wind speed.
  These improvements persist when the coarse input is changed from ERA5 to forecasts from the Aurora AI forecasting model, and when predicting at newly deployed stations with no regional history. To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [269] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化流程，使非专家用户能够为给定任务实例化并迭代人类对齐的奖励函数（即符合偏好排序的奖励函数）。该流程包含三个步骤：将自然语言描述的任务目标提炼为基本目标并推导可测量的结果变量、选择因果代表性的结果变量子集作为奖励项、通过偏好引导拟合奖励权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使非专家能够设计人类对齐的奖励函数，降低奖励函数设计的门槛，并确保奖励函数能真正反映用户的偏好排序。

Method: 流程包含三个步骤：(1) 引导式工作流，从自然语言任务描述中推导结果变量；(2) 将奖励项选择归结为因果DAG上的最小代价部分覆盖问题，通过最大流在多项式时间内求解；(3) 将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题，通过分离预言机（separation oracle）迭代缩小可行权重区域，仅需O(n log κ)次偏好查询。

Result: 该方法将奖励项选择与权重拟合进行了形式化，并首次实现了一种确定性无冲突的可行权重区域保持方法，可通过分离预言机将可行区域收敛到所需容差。

Conclusion: 这是首个在奖励设计过程中维持确定性无冲突可行权重区间并高效收敛的奖励设计方法，使非专家也能系统化地构建符合人类偏好的奖励函数。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [270] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RCI框架，将轨迹级的稀疏停止反馈（仅标记首个不安全转换）通过回报分解转化为稠密的逐步骤成本，从而在离线安全强化学习中实现时域信用分配。理论证明回报等价的再分配保持CMDP的可行策略集和最优拉格朗日量，实验表明其在违规率上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线安全RL通常假定可获得稠密的逐步骤成本标注，但实际中监督者只能提供轨迹级停止反馈——在首个不安全转换处的二元信号，缺乏逐步骤归因。这种时域信用分配问题限制了安全离线策略学习的应用。

Method: 提出RCI（基于再分配的成本推断）框架，通过回报分解将稀疏的停止反馈转换为稠密的逐步骤成本，然后在增强数据集上训练带约束的离线策略。核心在于利用回报等价的再分配保持CMDP的理论保真度。

Result: 理论证明回报等价的再分配保持可行策略集和最优拉格朗日量，转化在理论上是无损的，同时实践中使成本评论员学习条件更优。在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作实验上，违规率显著低于稀疏和基于分类器的基线，并对异质数据集组成和标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RCI框架有效解决了稀疏停止反馈下的安全离线RL问题，通过理论上无损的转化（保持CMDP可行策略集与最优拉格朗日量）将稀疏信号增强为稠密成本，在实验中展示了更低的违规率和更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


### [271] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时蒸馏：通过强模型构建推理时脚手架，在不更新参数的情况下帮助弱模型提升性能，在四个心智理论基准上将目标模型平均性能从0.49提升至0.91。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒸馏方法通常依赖训练时更新参数来将大模型能力迁移到小模型，本文探讨是否可以在测试时实现这种能力迁移，从而无需重新训练即可让强模型帮助弱模型更可靠地解决任务。

Method: 提出强到弱脚手架方法：使用强builder模型以5%数据作为验证集，通过多轮迭代优化推理时脚手架（harness），最终将确定化的harness在完整测试集上评估，而弱目标模型参数保持不变。

Result: 测试时能力迁移非常有效，目标模型平均性能从0.49翻倍至0.91。收益主要来自将不稳定推理卸载到确定性代码、基准特定路由和严格答案格式强制，而非促进更广泛的推理或采样。builder模型的推理努力单调提升harness质量，平台效应相对较小，越弱的目标模型获益越大。

Conclusion: 推理时脚手架设计是训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使强模型无需重训练即可将认知结构迁移至弱模型，为模型能力迁移提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>
